## 路线 & 巡礼之前

7月底的时候被朋友提醒有香港到四国德岛的便宜机票，往返800带行李额，虽然没有明确的目标，但还是先买了机票，之后再制定计划，我愿称之为 TDT(Ticket-Driven Travel)

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F89ee8f0e-8bbf-4be8-ad13-ed22fc090499%2F6728a244-c20b-4bb3-9298-6b98ba9f7813.png%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3Dc4cfcbbad9884cd9a5a4db1e845372fa4359914fbd737d98fcfd04b74acdc64f%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-808c-afe6-ffe706673c74&cache=v2&width=1400)

打开[巡礼地图](https://www.anitabi.cn/map)，在德岛附近第一眼看到的是 Summer Pockets，恰好最近动漫放送，决定趁着这个机会去一趟正式的圣地巡礼，目标确定

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2Fcaa206d0-b74f-4941-a6c4-bea660772b9f%2F21465ea3-48f1-4ccb-af10-70370e837425.png%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3Ddaad97b4df50ebc7ff79c218bb33796798f621c7639d70bec9cc9b5bd6af5485%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-800f-af8a-e82273005c23&cache=v2&width=1400)

德岛落地需要先去高松，JR特急路程一个小时。再从高松作为濑户内海跳岛旅的起点，其中直岛、男木岛和女木岛三座岛是 Summer Pockets 原作舞台中的主要取景地

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F16067859-979c-47d3-b93a-79c23be9788a%2Fimage.png%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3D60969168c324f06b7db49aeb356fbbc812d625af10aa057162545bfea015f00a%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-803e-81ab-dc09d9d5039e&cache=v2&width=1400)

上岛的唯一交通方式是渡轮，在船运公司的官网可以看到[ダイヤ（时刻表）](https://www.shikokukisen.com/instant/)和[买票方式](https://www.shikokukisen.com/stand/)，看到只能开船前半个小时在现场买票，反而放心了点（四国相对落后，如果要线上提前预定可能对没有日本银行卡和手机号的外国人不便）

住宿需要尽量提前订，很多民宿在一两个月前就会被预定完。可以选择住岛上，能有更原汁原味的体验，但价格相对更贵也更难预定。另一个方案是住高松，每天再坐船上岛，我选择了在高松的民宿，位于花園站附近（一直以为这个站读かえん，下车看到站牌才发现是はなぞの），去高松港很方便，平均价格250/晚。注意在小城市住宿时即使在车站周围也需要确认发车间隔，一些站因为客流过少可能一小时才发一班，出行会比较麻烦



最后，上岛后的攻略只用参考巡礼地图和官方（我不确定，也许是同人？）指南即可，每一个地点的位置都有标记

-   [巡礼地图 - 共享取景地标与截图 - 动画巡礼 - anitabi - 圣地巡礼 - 聖地巡礼 - せいちじゅんれい - 神奇海螺试验场](https://www.anitabi.cn/map)
-   [Summer Pockets – -サマーポケッツ- 聖地巡礼](https://summer-pockets.main.jp/)



出发当天，如果不在香港过夜要从深圳出发只能走24h的皇岗口岸过关，到达机场时刚好日出。作为冷门航线，这趟航班人意外地多，据说是香港-德岛航线在8月底将会关停，所以有不少人专程来坐最后几趟

*香港机场的日出*

*廉航惯例边缘登机口*

德岛机场很小，航站楼还没深圳很多地铁站大，国际航班只有香港和韩国仁川两条航线（其中一条还即将关停）。为了吸引游客，机场会免费发放 BUS PASS 给国际线的乘客（仅限外国人），可以在两天内免费乘坐所有市内巴士，也包括机场巴士，可以直达德岛站

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F5e79e28d-1d60-46b5-879b-6cf5aa60ec60%2FIMG_0697.jpeg%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466UQ5UNQLE%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140431Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJIMEYCIQCvmjcPC71%252FN4XQKr7zjO4AJoNuaOIb4gLtyJQu5x4JdQIhAOq1idISabiyjeKhU1moZQaqn%252BAcxlz4OrAv58kzgihQKogECIz%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEQABoMNjM3NDIzMTgzODA1IgwUP3gEBR%252BNKCd9Pfwq3AMmCXx0qJ29CPDw11QmKYiJG1UV%252BSlLP4mL6Jn1tr14HGqJweFabIM9oLqBxvAIdIz%252BGnxTlgoT4V1nhBVzS0C0ppzC4DUl98VYzJHzbIMIkZ89bHZugfB5RtMfliqlHE0H6DVga6W1CVY1s8N5icynqNXYFkWbQXAfHoKRj6qJ%252Fd4fD0n0gmBPQprIUJoN%252FfuCJAAvCpyoJVxEvtw1eDiaQnpI9hP1ia38Uo2VL0NllGjNFBCbV5T5KYQn13XX1Gwt4PjbDVOratyqe%252BpegSFQeaCNLUrles3fpLoA%252Bi9vTMcB1FF%252Fe0PNXsYC%252FPcxCw7Jx%252BYp2sd%252BqJpMmao02rh3zr7i7u9qi6NzVyiMmta23EicXPAmZjqEyE%252FBjm3owRgLRoOV%252BBBZ8vXmwgZR2o276C1gkuK3%252FRBQFQsqyt93SR8DqUsznp9iEjNzGdq3uhLgcawYxpjYfSGPiFMHKNI4gz8%252BaioCy9eIcwdysjGVFIzphC0jphfEC6mJ8WvsOOCi3zK%252BWv0rCmljjWouDbhBZqAujYMixuGpNwm4VDfvLCdyXoBy1XJh3Mk3nbgfnfCdzjDv3w9x1nWOq7mTteDk2U%252FIWeTw96SA9YXXZhzPPxiotdSIEGm9oLl0EjDVpsTVBjqkAZltnlaqpmYHqnCCXGTWTConoWdcrYNtp5%252FHiDbSuN3E8YcIVHpfpIyi5Iej6EJx4d%252BUDgLtep%252BVHAipgkuuYxwO%252F0Sa5ogjixXwT85Vn7ZB8b%252BR0LaNBS5%252FcccWxTiRKfVNU59ldu%252B33J%252Bl0fRLfsGGriJz%252FNGjwiLpJxOpWEo3olLy6S3wENyeFngc8VIEq3ABf2RZK%252FjvCRv39EgPa1Xsf8LW%26X-Amz-Signature%3D0f4535dd433430c0f4d5c707fcc21439c04b12c9d8afc827103d8e3ece9119d1%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-80c6-97ab-e322c3aa631c&cache=v2&width=1400)

第一晚在德岛度过，顺便去看了鬼灭，德岛还有唯一一个 ufotable CINEMA，但场次比较少没有合适的时间，最后去了永旺，刚好赶上 23 号的第三弹新特典

在德岛能很明显感受到这座小城的老龄化和人口流失，除了车站和永旺这类购物中心外，其他地方的人流量都非常少，商店街也看不到几个人，大部分店铺处于关停状态

第二天出发前往高松，相比德岛，高松的人多了不少，也许是因为恰逢濑户内国际艺术祭，有很多游客，车站里的书店也摆满了宣传

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F6990e83b-8d32-4c6e-bdbe-8d561d92e3aa%2FIMG_0781.jpeg%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3De47e0fe7a9c435540b73c6d483e5d43bf2e483b1f9e2d1caca6adfa560148de1%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-8029-b91a-feba29d7380a&cache=v2&width=1400)





## 直岛

正式上岛是第三天，在高松站出站后，左侧能看到高松港旅客终端大楼（高松港旅客ターミナルビル），站前广场也有明显的高松港引导路线。穿过终端大楼再往港口方向走，左侧1/2号登船口是直岛方向，右侧3/4号登船口是男木岛/女木岛方向，红色砖房也是售票处所在

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F1835b339-beb3-4543-bbac-5b1cd3a86075%2FDSC_1157.jpg%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466XS5H4LYL%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140431Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIQC1xEp3CA1ZhuL1dha4TOqYQ%252F4y8afnoJ9latkLUUTvHQIgFewc7xJtS%252B6bf70KL3%252FzmtT3s8MaWidy30%252Bs5EZmYjkqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDGsNziVwP4%252FkMeL6vircAwjU8xLHbgMHVSpcqcLxj5ZC1ddIDOx%252BysXkRRSzu3OoFkTVHhVcEcnQu1glLLO8TN0Qu1elRF5gE%252Bkjl4THEZHJfgxzSemOmNEQWQdcTSMbW2kCxpNpOjlPCgoHKrqhASfCT3JifWAVbSUvZcBNSdNWsjeJDjLDyPf90DCDdYHvarppUtOQI2WLsUKj%252FQFPxiO10pmQh9d4q5zYyCzYIWYAajS32wdc557tUioxQ3nVKUaiP3eLyRZs43EdAPnlbY2vSM4DodSJROOZ3KowNVD8LL6xDjbax3WqAAWi5hgGnkQT4bsOtIW1xPW7H68vAvviTxcD3yPYsmcfl7EWUlRGYSN7UCvXVb2wQk5PltmRCX1HELealqosvKvotAPPoH94pQlGKiEGd0qDCmYY7P%252BKNt5HRTyxFHarmW7NnGTT5GR7zJgBsXMMwM%252BQYWSfJJxYk3JbvLWuhcyqdLwwBM3B1vXMGR7emr1w%252BkoJ6L7E%252BhaowJyoc7%252FUiA3l99do%252FsylESgXvV18xUn%252FShYSMnya6H%252BAg5PNZZCWiSNIOJ0SqoqAlWsobc%252F8Kyq4eM4aJYYy52DEFoo53nEIkEHkAeZx18JF4TZlwKjM1ORFHRObhZ4z%252B7RFD4Kk4anXMPimxNUGOqUBpOoVYgi%252FcDlpIVQ%252FVeVTTPYiqUBno1X2J%252BSEvv%252BnHQlHYNUoqAekC8tHkIWBGGZAMyQmPTUI4xq%252BSpXXv6shEvZh4kItQeLMEZynDGPhaUsueywzXCoQ3JtIj2ja3CgB7gK9YnYqoiSOzDmYehw0%252BTJHqZiO17D%252FOnIQyKdRcHHmFSpBriL4sDQygWacQWDnoGuHAnmXN0FmlEIcfhoYtjahhKfE%26X-Amz-Signature%3D1a6ee4823f10dab4d0f0cddf9ab91f44cee218e033c338d5296cca3bc07899a5%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26188756-3979-80b1-a51f-ccfe5564452c&cache=v2&width=1400)

售票处内自动售票机可以直接买不定班次的往返票（去程票当日有效，返程票7日内有效），船分为渡轮（フェリー）和高速旅客船，班次和时间都不一样，具体可以看网站上的[时刻表](https://www.shikokukisen.com/instant/)

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2Ffcf65ff8-b433-4b0f-b85e-264c3a1488ff%2F683f5f74-d9e8-473d-8b77-8405f124c0af.png%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3D61ed04b9c29b23771d95f5ba2bfbf9345acb9296120608e84c0795a1402da903%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-804f-a6d0-e7fb584f7d03&cache=v2&width=1400)

最好乘坐一早9点或10点的的班次，渡轮位置很多，我这趟没有坐满，行程大约50分钟

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2Fb93e9ef3-32a2-4f88-989c-318307eff07c%2FIMG_0759.jpeg%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3D42f6a51383ee8601b4855be7806adaf5432c0334896dd2c4e65aff7f8d42770c%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-8066-875c-f26c33dd543d&cache=v2&width=1400)



目的地的直岛宫浦港就是原作内的鸟白岛港口，下船后走到右侧案内所前回头，拍下第一个巡礼点。不用特别急，船至少会停几十分钟

（通过观察下船的游客是直冲毒蘑菇一样的红色大南瓜还是来这个机位拍照，可以准确分辨来艺术祭的文艺男和来巡礼的死宅

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F992ab239-4c66-4355-addb-b67343c2e9ce%2FIMG_0787.jpg%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3Df2ad36c1600b7aef047ea0ab9c56882c291b7b7fddce8044c25d24ecc65b4274%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26088756-3979-8074-8c41-d214395ff5c2&cache=v2&width=1400)

穿过案内所，对面是能租到胡子猫团自行车的租车店，据说在店长耳边低语サマポケ的暗号能收获一本店长做的巡礼指南。可惜也许是因为非假期的原因，当天没开门，最后去了另一家租车店租了带电助力的车

理论上也可以徒步走，但直岛不小，如果要在一天内巡礼完，徒步会很折磨。另外务必做好防晒措施，8月的濑户内海紫外线指数非常高，只需要半小时就足够晒伤皮肤（现身说法

到这里港口周围就巡礼得差不多了（其实还有个神社，但貌似是三菱的企业私有领地不能入内，建议遵循ルール不要擅自闯入)。听说711有スイカバー能买，但我没找到，只能继续往东边探索，这里是一条大路联通东西两侧

在半路右转可以到达水坝，经过一段即使有电助力也会比较吃力的大坡度上坡后，是白羽发射れいだーん的场地。这里如果要下去到平台的话，貌似要走大坝内部，不确定是否能进去；也有一些人从旁边的坡跨下去，需要注意安全

原路返回后继续一路向东，慢慢进入居民区，注意到道路左侧有家石井商店，是民宿和乌冬面馆，在原作里这里是羽依里家，站在门前侧方的路上可以得到同机位

（其实前面还有白羽家，被我漏掉了

民宿门口也和原作里羽依里家的CG相同，面馆内则是原作里的食堂。不过当时面馆内没人，貌似不在营业中，也不是民宿住客，就不好进去拍摄了。另外这个面馆也将于年底停业，还想巡礼的需要抓紧时间了

后面有个小屋子的地方是白羽钓鱼的位置，这里只能在退潮时走左侧岸边过去，涨潮时间可以在[直島港(香川県)の潮汐](https://www.umitenki.jp/tenki/3873/tide)上看到。当时海水已经把岸边淹了没能过去（其实翻岩壁还是能去的，不过安全起见没敢走），比较遗憾

下面的沙滩也是原作的经典场地，现实中因为挨着海水浴场人会很多，不过我以为只能从隔壁的住宿区进入（禁止非住客入内），没有拍到，实际直接从沙滩走过去就好

直岛的主要巡礼点到这里都已经去过了，要注意虽然海水浴场离港口直线距离很近，但这段路不能骑车通过，即使下车推也不行（不知道为什么会有这个规定），只能原路绕个圈回到港口



## 男木岛

次日继续前往男木岛，男木岛的售票处在3/4号登船口，是另一个方向，并且没有自动售票机，只能人工买票

这趟船从高松港出发，中途在女木岛停船，最后到达男木岛。女木岛的主要巡礼点是羽依里和欧探索的洞穴、以及通向秘密基地的山道，由于船的班次不多，要在一天内探索男木岛和女木岛时间会比较紧张，最后没有去女木岛

![Image](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2Fac603f13-565d-43b2-b2f3-c5be523bf5f2%2Fimage.png%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3D317b29afb39b862a61acd2298d17aec679e4ec3942f848f7cdb5142bd1bf946b%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26188756-3979-80e0-9d1f-e2562fd0b716&cache=v2&width=1400)

相比直岛，男木岛航线的船相对较小，位置会不太够，但也不需要坐下，中途有重要巡礼点，建议上船后直接走到船头等待

![这个方向其实是船尾，靠岸时船头朝向岸边，登船铁板会降下来](https://www.notion.so/image/https%3A%2F%2Fprod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com%2F4cc04375-345a-4a1e-bdf0-3a7c88ef0425%2F3582733e-d5a6-4b63-838f-30e3accaff85%2FDSC_1163_726CF7244C7A_CAMERAMARK.jpeg%3FX-Amz-Algorithm%3DAWS4-HMAC-SHA256%26X-Amz-Content-Sha256%3DUNSIGNED-PAYLOAD%26X-Amz-Credential%3DASIAZI2LB466WPQCONIW%252F20260921%252Fus-west-2%252Fs3%252Faws4_request%26X-Amz-Date%3D20260921T140429Z%26X-Amz-Expires%3D3600%26X-Amz-Security-Token%3DIQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEMT%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FwEaCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJHMEUCIG6nuR7ytB6D%252F%252BZbUqDC1roSKmpPnz23JTFMq6X2CuIiAiEA8s1J0exYusuzjaGznTKmsx%252BmSHVc4WfvOcGt%252B%252BNQ6kMqiAQIjP%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252F%252FARAAGgw2Mzc0MjMxODM4MDUiDH5V657wxtw8EKf69ircA82imjyvoa3DFXXAB9Rly%252FxtGCkdA1m1g1cGmg05aPb43MnI4dbDwI%252BuiO5ZyJpjVDKqUz5W%252BIFl6F0B%252B8NXCOgGhrMoQpa1EBHFuRCFqrr%252FV60CVOKrZa%252BaMvAahmNncYSmD73AtWkHHHmTKvfnCiClmBAYppo%252BdHy6PNnyxDBEJs9CIqy%252BhhwULiWAb7UEHk0Hog%252FW7CuBTExjQNbyOXeCWLBp5c2aYzHp00ffp9ALJTw51a30fRf6Uhp0yZahgrewkhbMoSGo0X8sw15WKB3Fxy6LHRex7L%252FlRL6HkJI01w99sPiSVe3TMk5GjrNS%252BsN1IQlRRbcz1LDOd%252BGemoKTt2kuUNvSnm7IK1QSRAxdNa72v2VbnYOziCnY81mBuNRNTVS14CxwQAOj1ytl21VJ0MBzKAN5op2MBrdTo4btn3Gdhv7pwyg7JaA0XUB9%252BlV3PT05h7NY6fQgAgAxzbUeaBR7LIUnZeWcNHczKE8hHI5uxtCE72NMOlJADORjM8N%252B1xtbY9mEAgTFU2nFXqpdsGV5pmQ6jpnyQa2zCcXPXYNpGcZCjYCm8Go51A%252BfMK%252FykUckn7Nr25Nttr3YpRom2pfQFtHYB5CCaRXWpR4jGsW9hgQBHYaVFk%252BhMPSmxNUGOqUBbzB3%252FQjBweZg8whUA4uu7nsBuFD%252BmidFJjV1FVIV0qDmdLvpE66LtSw%252Bg55b2o8JmR7h0KML88pz2E4uLkEy%252F9EmFhXcMcPlzCJ3DoPJsa2Pr2BFAh29ryROFU9209gs58mfh7mdmeLljHzZkuhvNOqo6jKZyvnumCldatcDP3erxX4O1MmVOnp7rbMWjXTatWCgJKfmLTCemvNfCf0y4rnmufYT%26X-Amz-Signature%3D012957b1d817dde0b9ca7d64561d1f288a05bc0c6d67155e280f6045056f2e46%26X-Amz-SignedHeaders%3Dhost%26x-amz-checksum-mode%3DENABLED%26x-id%3DGetObject?table=block&id=26188756-3979-80ea-ba39-ce081c9edc5e&cache=v2&width=1400)
*这个方向其实是船尾，靠岸时船头朝向岸边，登船铁板会降下来*

从女木岛再次启程后5分钟左右，原作开头的鸟白岛全景就在眼前，拍摄窗口只有一分钟，错过了也没关系，返程时在船尾还能再拍一次

男木岛不大，可以徒步巡礼完，不需要租车。下船后右侧直走是鸟白岛役场，中途能看到不太起眼的广播塔（有好好穿着衣服，没人拿水枪滋我

门口摆满了关于岛上的猫（男木岛因为猫很多也称为猫岛，虽然我并没有看到太多）和 Summer Pockets 的各种周边

旁边是丰爷爷的家门口，大门敞开说明正在家中，欢迎所有巡礼者进去休息和交流，还有机会蹭饭（但我太社恐没敢进去）

继续往南走大约50米，有一所小学，栏杆内是白羽游泳的泳池，注意栏杆前的板子写着禁止摄影（撮影できません），还是继续遵循ルール不要拍照了，特别是有人的时候。原本学校旁还有原作里和基友打乒乓球的迷你基地，不过早已拆除

之后的路都比较原生态，会有不少虫子和蜘蛛。只有路旁不时出现的路牌提醒我们离灯台的距离在不断缩短

男木岛的巡礼到这里就结束了，虽然岛不大，但完全靠走也需要大半天

回去港口时注意提前预留时间，最后一班船在下午5点，错过就只能在岛上过夜了

## 写在最后

这趟行程最初本来想找朋友一起去，因为时间安排关系没能找到人陪同，全程一个人规划和出发（实际上solo trip的体验非常好），最后也算顺利完成，圆了自己一个小心愿

一路上碰到了不少国内和日本的同好（太社恐了没什么交流hhh），感觉大家都很开心，虽然行程比较仓促，也有不少东西被漏掉或者没能好好体验，但旅途中的未知和遗憾也是过程的一部分吧，希望下次能继续去更多地方巡礼

作为初次正式的圣地巡礼，献给了 Summer Pockets，在现实中到访ACG作品里出现过的场景时，仿佛真的能走进那段故事里，和他们度过相同的夏天，是一种难以言喻的跨越次元的感动

最后，用原作里的台词结束吧

眩しさだけは、忘れなかった
